# Interaction inventory

Generated by scripts/document-portal-inventory.mjs from every interactive JSX control declaration and call site in domains/. Shared controls and dynamic repeated controls are included as templates; their exact labels, routes and domain transition choices come from the route registry, mapped arrays and administrative schemas. Source locations identify each implementation. Native field options, repeated rows, tabs and schema commands inherit their owning template's behavior.

The test column distinguishes component/domain assertions from route smoke coverage; it does not claim a dedicated assertion for every field option. Backend endpoint families and invariants are in API_CONTRACT.md.

528 control declarations/call sites catalogued across 106 portal route patterns.

| Route                                                                                                           | User role                           | Control label                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | Component                                             | Required permission                                    | Action                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | Confirmation required                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | Success result                                                 | Failure result                                                                   | Loading state                                        | Empty state                                                                            | Backend dependency                                  | Test covering it                                                                 |
